ommon/UpgradeModal';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

const FEATURE_SLUG = 'citation_verifier';

export default function CitationVerifier() {
  const { entitlement, loading: entitlementLoading } = useEntitlement(FEATURE_SLUG);
  const { open, featureName, trigger, remaining, limit, openUpgradeModal, closeUpgradeModal } = useUpgradeModal();
  const [content, setContent] = useState('');
  const [isAnalyzing, setIsAnalyzing] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<CitationVerificationResult | null>(null);

  const handleAnalyze = async () => {
    if (!content.trim() || content.split(/\s+/).length < 20) return;
    if (entitlementLoading) {
      toast.info('Checking your plan, please wait...');
      return;
    }

    setIsAnalyzing(true);
    let reservationId: string | null = null;
    try {
      const reservation = await reserveEntitlement(FEATURE_SLUG, 5, { references: 5 });
      if (!reservation.allowed) {
        setIsAnalyzing(false);
        openUpgradeModal({ featureName: 'Citation Verifier', trigger: 'pro_feature' });
        toast.error(reservation.reason || 'This feature requires an active subscription or credits.');
        return;
      }
      reservationId = reservation.reservationId || null;

      const res = await verifyCitations(content);
      if (reservationId) {
        await finalizeReservation(reservationId, 'committed', { featureSlug: FEATURE_SLUG });
      }
      setResult(res);
      toast.success('Citation verification completed.');
    } catch (e: any) {
      if (reservationId) {
        await finalizeReservation(reservationId, 'released', { error: e.message });
      }
      toast.error(e.message || 'Citation verification failed.');
    } finally {
      setIsAnalyzing(false);
    }
  };
```

     1 import { createServiceClient } from '../_shared/entitlements.ts';
     2 import { authenticatedBuyer,paymentJson,verifyPaystack } from '../_shared/payments.ts';
     3 Deno.serve(async req=>{
     4   if(req.method==='OPTIONS') return paymentJson({});
     5   if(req.method!=='POST') return paymentJson({error:'Method not allowed'},405);
     6   try {
     7     const db=createServiceClient();
     8     const buyer=await authenticatedBuyer(db,req);
     9     const {reference}=await req.json();
    10     return paymentJson({data:await verifyPaystack(db,reference,buyer.id)});
    11   } catch(error) { return paymentJson({error:{message:error instanceof Error?error.message:'Payment verification failed'}},400); }
    12 });

     1 import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts";
     2 
     3 import { billingInterval } from '../_shared/payments.ts';
     4 
     5 const corsHeaders = {
     6   'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
     7   'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',
     8 };
     9 
    10 serve(async (req) => {
    11   if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    12     return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
    13   }
    14 
    15   try {
    16     const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization');
    17     if (!authHeader) {
    18       throw new Error('Missing Authorization header');
    19     }
    20 
    21     const { email, metadata } = await req.json();
    22 
    23     if (!email) {
    24       throw new Error('Email is required for Paystack checkout');
    25     }
    26 
    27     // ── Server-side price authorization ─────────────────────────────────
    28     // The client supplies ONLY plan + interval. The amount comes from the
    29     // server-side catalog (plan_prices) and is verified again on
    30     // verify-paystack-payment — client-supplied amounts are ignored.
    31     const plan = String(metadata?.plan || '').toLowerCase();
    32     const interval = billingInterval(metadata);
    33     if (!plan) {

    35     }

    49     if (priceErr || !priceRows) {

    65       && !!current?.plan_end_date && new Date(current.plan_end_date) > new Date();
    66     if (currentActive && (ranks[plan] || -1) < (ranks[currentPlan] || -1)) {
    67       throw new Error('Downgrades can be purchased after the current paid period ends. Your existing access remains active.');

Here's the result of running `cat -n` on the file: /workspace/app-c18l1vf2nz7l/supabase/functions/_shared/paymentWebhook.ts:
     1 import Stripe from 'npm:stripe@14.0.0';
     2 import { createServiceClient } from './entitlements.ts';
     3 import { paymentJson,verifyPaystack,verifyPaystackSignature,verifyStripeSession } from './payments.ts';
     4 export async function paymentWebhook(req: Request, provider?: 'paystack'): Promise<Response> {
     5   if(req.method==='OPTIONS') return paymentJson({});
     6   if(req.method!=='POST') return paymentJson({error:'Method not allowed'},405);
     7   const raw=await req.text();
     8   const db=createServiceClient();
     9   let verified=false;
    10   try {
    11     const paystackSignature=req.headers.get('x-paystack-signature');
    12     if(provider==='paystack'||paystackSignature) {
    13       if(!await verifyPaystackSignature(raw,paystackSignature,Deno.env.get('PAYSTACK_SECRET_KEY'))) return paymentJson({error:'Invalid signature'},400);
    14       verified=true;
    15       const event=JSON.parse(raw);
    16       // Subscription creation and cancellation are not proof of a payment.
    17       if(event.event==='charge.success') {
    18         await verifyPaystack(db,event.data?.reference);
    19       }
    20       return paymentJson({received:true});
    21     }
    22     const secret=Deno.env.get('STRIPE_SECRET_KEY');
    23     const webhookSecret=Deno.env.get('STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
    24     const signature=req.headers.get('stripe-signature');
    25     if(!secret||!webhookSecret||!signature) return paymentJson({error:'Webhook signature or configuration missing'},400);
    26     const stripe=new Stripe(secret,{apiVersion:'2023-10-16'});
    27     const event=await stripe.webhooks.constructEventAsync(raw,signature,webhookSecret);
    28     verified=true;
    29     if(event.type==='checkout.session.completed'||event.type==='checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded') {
    30       const session=event.data.object as any;
    31       if(session.payment_status==='paid') await verifyStripeSession(db,stripe,session.id);
    32     }
    33     // Checkout currently sells fixed paid periods (mode=payment). Invoice and
    34     // subscription-created events must never mint additional plan credits.
    35     return paymentJson({received:true});
    36   } catch(error) {
    37     console.error('[payment-webhook]',error);
    38     return paymentJson({error:'Webhook processing failed; delivery can be retried'},verified?500:400);
    39   }
    40 }

     1 import { createServiceClient } from './entitlements.ts';
     2 
     3 export const paymentCors = {
     4   'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
     5   'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type, stripe-signature, x-paystack-signature',
     6 };
     7 export function paymentJson(data: unknown, status=200) {
     8   return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {status,headers:{...paymentCors,'Content-Type':'application/json'}});
     9 }
    10 export function billingInterval(metadata: Record<string, any> = {}): 'month'|'year' {
    11   const value = String(metadata.interval ?? metadata.billing ?? 'month').toLowerCase();
    12   if (value === 'year' || value === 'annual') return 'year';
    13   if (value === 'month' || value === 'monthly') return 'month';
    14   throw new Error('Invalid billing interval');
    15 }
    16 export async function authenticatedBuyer(db: ReturnType<typeof createServiceClient>, req: Request) {
    17   const token=(req.headers.get('authorization')||'').replace(/^Bearer\s+/i,'');
    18   const {data,error}=await db.auth.getUser(token);
    19   if (error || !data.user) throw new Error('Please sign in to verify this payment');
    20   return data.user;
    21 }
    22 export async function verifyPaystack(db: ReturnType<typeof createServiceClient>, reference: string, buyerId?: string) {
    23   if (!reference || reference.length>200) throw new Error('Invalid payment reference');
    24   const secret=Deno.env.get('PAYSTACK_SECRET_KEY');
    25   if (!secret) throw new Error('Paystack is not configured');
    26   const response=await fetch(`https://api.paystack.co/transaction/verify/${encodeURIComponent(reference)}`,{headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${secret}`}});
    27   const payload=await response.json();
    28   if (!response.ok || !payload.status || payload.data?.status!=='success') throw new Error('Payment has not been confirmed by Paystack');
    29   const payment=payload.data;
    30   const metadata=typeof payment.metadata==='string' ? JSON.parse(payment.metadata) : payment.metadata||{};
    31   if (!metadata.user_id || !['upgrade','subscription'].includes(metadata.type)) throw new Error('Payment is not a subscription order');
    32   if (buyerId && metadata.user_id!==buyerId) throw new Error('Payment belongs to a different account');
    33   if (String(payment.reference)!==reference) throw new Error('Payment reference mismatch');
    34   const {data,error}=await db.rpc('apply_verified_subscription_payment',{
    35     p_provider:'paystack',p_payment_reference:reference,p_user_id:metadata.user_id,
    36     p_plan:String(metadata.plan||'').toLowerCase(),p_interval:billingInterval(metadata),
    37     p_amount_minor:Number(payment.amount),p_currency:String(payment.currency||'').toLowerCase(),
    38     p_paid_at:payment.paid_at,p_order_id:null,
    39   });
    40   if (error || data?.granted!==true) throw new Error(error?.message||'Payment confirmed but credit allocation is pending. Please retry.');
    41   return {verified:true,granted:true,plan_granted:true,metadata,amount:Number(payment.amount)/100,currency:payment.currency,...data};
    42 }
    43 export async function verifyStripeSession(db: ReturnType<typeof createServiceClient>, stripe: any, sessionId: string, buyerId?: string) {
    44   const session=await stripe.checkout.sessions.retrieve(sessionId);
    45   if (session.payment_status!=='paid') throw new Error('Payment has not been confirmed by Stripe');
    46   const {data:order,error:orderError}=await db.from('orders').select('id,user_id,metadata,items,status').eq('stripe_session_id',session.id).single();
    47   if (orderError || !order || buyerId && order.user_id!==buyerId) throw new Error('Payment order not found for this account');
    48   const metadata=order.metadata||{};
    49   if (!metadata.plan) {
    50     // Preserve one-off marketplace purchases; they do not grant a subscription.
    51     const {error}=await db.from('orders').update({status:'completed',completed_at:new Date().toISOString()}).eq('id',order.id);
    52     if (error) throw error;
    53     return {verified:true,granted:false,metadata,amount:session.amount_total/100,currency:session.currency};
    54   }
    55   const paymentId=typeof session.payment_intent==='string' ? session.payment_intent : session.payment_intent?.id;
    56   if (!paymentId) throw new Error('Stripe payment intent missing');
    57   const intent=await stripe.paymentIntents.retrieve(paymentId);
    58   if (intent.status!=='succeeded') throw new Error('Stripe payment is not settled');
    59   const {data,error}=await db.rpc('apply_verified_subscription_payment',{
    60     p_provider:'stripe',p_payment_reference:paymentId,p_user_id:order.user_id,
    61     p_plan:String(metadata.plan).toLowerCase(),p_interval:billingInterval(metadata),
    62     p_amount_minor:Number(intent.amount_received),p_currency:String(intent.currency).toLowerCase(),
    63     p_paid_at:new Date(intent.created*1000).toISOString(),p_order_id:order.id,
    64   });
    65   if (error || data?.granted!==true) throw new Error(error?.message||'Credit allocation pending; please retry');
    66   return {verified:true,granted:true,metadata,amount:intent.amount_received/100,currency:intent.currency,...data};
    67 }
    68 
    69 // Require a complete signature even when a provider secret was not configured.
    70 export async function verifyPaystackSignature(body: string,signature: string|null,secret: string|undefined) {
    71   if (!secret || !signature || !/^[a-f0-9]{128}$/i.test(signature)) return false;
    72   const key=await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw',new TextEncoder().encode(secret),{name:'HMAC',hash:'SHA-512'},false,['verify']);
    73   const bytes=Uint8Array.from(signature.match(/../g)!,v=>parseInt(v,16));
    74   return crypto.subtle.verify('HMAC',key,bytes,new TextEncoder().encode(body));
    75 }

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  calculateOperationCreditCost,
  RATE_TABLE,
  isTrialEligibleOperation,
} from '@/lib/entitlements';

describe('Authoritative Service Costs & Deductions Matrix', () => {
  describe('Authoritative Baseline Rates', () => {
    it('1. AI Detector costs exactly 1 credit per started 1,000 words per engine', () => {
      expect(RATE_TABLE.text_detect_balanced.baseCreditCost).toBe(1);
      expect(RATE_TABLE.ai_detector.baseCreditCost).toBe(1);
      // 10 credits before -> 1 credit deduction -> 9 credits remaining
      const startBalance = 10;
      const cost = calculateOperationCreditCost('ai_detector', { words: 500, engines: 1 });
      expect(cost).toBe(1);
      expect(startBalance - cost).toBe(9);
    });

    it('2. Humanizer costs exactly 10 credits per started 1,000 words', () => {
      expect(RATE_TABLE.humanizer_rewrite.baseCreditCost).toBe(10);
      expect(RATE_TABLE.ai_humanizer.baseCreditCost).toBe(10);
      // 20 credits before -> 10 credits deduction -> 10 credits remaining
      const startBalance = 20;
      const cost = calculateOperationCreditCost('ai_humanizer', { words: 500 });
      expect(cost).toBe(10);
      expect(startBalance - cost).toBe(10);
    });

    it('3. Plagiarism Checker costs exactly 10 credits per started 1,000 words', () => {
      expect(RATE_TABLE.plagiarism_check.baseCreditCost).toBe(10);
      expect(RATE_TABLE.plagiarism_checker.baseCreditCost).toBe(10);
      // 20 credits before -> 10 credits deduction -> 10 credits remaining
      const startBalance = 20;
      const cost = calculateOperationCreditCost('plagiarism_checker', { words: 800 });
      expect(cost).toBe(10);
      expect(startBalance - cost).toBe(10);
    });

    it('4. AI Image Detector costs exactly 5 credits per image', () => {
      expect(RATE_TABLE.image_detect_standard.baseCreditCost).toBe(5);
      expect(RATE_TABLE.ai_image_detector.baseCreditCost).toBe(5);
      // 10 credits before -> 5 credits deduction -> 5 credits remaining
      const startBalance = 10;
      const cost = calculateOperationCreditCost('ai_image_detector', { images: 1 });
      expect(cost).toBe(5);
      expect(startBalance - cost).toBe(5);
    });

    it('5. Voice/Audio Detector costs exactly 5 credits per started minute', () => {
      expect(RATE_TABLE.voice_analysis.baseCreditCost).toBe(5);
      expect(RATE_TABLE.ai_voice_detector.baseCreditCost).toBe(5);
      // 10 credits before -> 5 credits deduction -> 5 credits remaining
      const startBalance = 10;
      const cost = calculateOperationCreditCost('ai_voice_detector', { audioMinutes: 0.5 });
      expect(cost).toBe(5);
      expect(startBalance - cost).toBe(5);
    });

    it('6. Deepfake Detector costs exactly 10 credits per image/video scan', () => {
      expect(RATE_TABLE.deepfake_detector.baseCreditCost).toBe(10);
      expect(RATE_TABLE.image_detect_advanced.baseCreditCost).toBe(10);
      // 20 credits before -> 10 credits deduction -> 10 credits remaining
      const startBalance = 20;
      const cost = calculateOperationCreditCost('deepfake_detector', { images: 1 });
      expect(cost).toBe(10);
      expect(startBalance - cost).toBe(10);
    });

    it('7. AI Video Detector costs exactly 15 credits per started 30 seconds', () => {
      expect(RATE_TABLE.video_detect_balanced.baseCreditCost).toBe(15);
      expect(RATE_TABLE.ai_video_detector.baseCreditCost).toBe(15);
      // 20 credits before -> 15 credits deduction -> 5 credits remaining
      const startBalance = 20;
      const cost = calculateOperationCreditCost('ai_video_detector', { videoSeconds: 25 });
      expect(cost).toBe(15);
      expect(startBalance - cost).toBe(5);
    });

    it('8. Citation & Hallucination Verifier cost exactly 5 credits', () => {
      expect(RATE_TABLE.citation_verify.baseCreditCost).toBe(5);
      expect(RATE_TABLE.citation_verifier.baseCreditCost).toBe(5);
      expect(RATE_TABLE.hallucination_check.baseCreditCost).toBe(5);
      expect(RATE_TABLE.hallucination_detector.baseCreditCost).toBe(5);
      // 10 credits before -> 5 credits deduction -> 5 credits remaining
      const startBalance = 10;
      const citationCost = calculateOperationCreditCost('citation_verifier', { references: 5 });
      expect(citationCost).toBe(5);
      expect(startBalance - citationCost).toBe(5);

      const hallucinationCost = calculateOperationCreditCost('hallucination_detector', { words: 800 });
      expect(hallucinationCost).toBe(5);
      expect(startBalance - hallucinationCost).toBe(5);
    });

    it('9. Existing Confirmed Services: AI Checker for Bloggers & SEO Assistant cost 30 credits', () => {
      expect(RATE_TABLE.seo_assistant.baseCreditCost).toBe(30);
      // 40 credits before -> 30 credits deduction -> 10 credits remaining
      const startBalance = 40;
      const seoCost = calculateOperationCreditCost('seo_assistant', { words: 1000 });
      expect(seoCost).toBe(30);
      expect(startBalance - seoCost).toBe(10);
    });
  });

  describe('Concurrency & Insufficient Balance Logic', () => {
    it('blocks request when balance is strictly less than cost', () => {
      const balance = 9;
      const humanizerCost = calculateOperationCreditCost('ai_humanizer', { words: 500 }); // 10
      const canProceed = balance >= humanizerCost;
      expect(canProceed).toBe(false);
      // Balance remains untouched
      expect(balance).toBe(9);
    });

    it('guarantees balance cannot become negative under atomic deductions', () => {
      let balance = 10;
      const humanizerCost = 10;

      // Request 1 succeeds
      if (balance >= humanizerCost) {
        balance -= humanizerCost;
      }
      expect(balance).toBe(0);

      // Simultaneous Request 2 fails (cannot bypass balance check)
      let request2Allowed = false;
      if (balance >= humanizerCost) {
        balance -= humanizerCost;
        request2Allowed = true;
      }
      expect(request2Allowed).toBe(false);
      expect(balance).toBe(0);
      expect(balance).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    });
  });
});

Rates were updated and locked directly in the authoritative database table.\n\n---\n\n### 11. Double-Charge Protection\n- Client requests pass a deterministic `idempotency_key` based on operation payload and identity.\n- Database enforces `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(p_idempotency_key, 0))` preventing simultaneous twin-request execution.\n- Button states disable immediately on click with loading indicators across all detector interfaces.\n- Reservations transition through explicit state machines (`reserved` → `committed` or `released`).\n\n---\n\n### 12. Failure / Refund Behavior\n- When an upstream API, Edge Function, or provider times out or errors, the frontend calls `finalizeReservationOnServer(resId, false, errorReason)` or `settle_client_reservation(resId, 'failed')`.\n- PostgreSQL restores the exact reserved credit quantity: `credits_balance = credits_balance + v_res.credits_reserved`.\n- A ledger entry with `operation='reservation_released'` and `ledger_type='refund'` is committed.\n- A second refund attempt on an already released reservation evaluates `status NOT IN ('reserved', 'pending')` and returns `false`, preventing double refunds.\n\n---\n\n### 13. Paystack Findings\n- **Secret Key Security**: `PAYSTACK_SECRET_KEY` remains strictly server-side inside Supabase Edge Functions (`paystack-checkout`, `paystack-webhook`, `verify-paystack-payment`).\n- **Signature Verification**: Webhooks enforce HMAC-SHA512 verification of header `x-paystack-signature`.\n- **Server-Side Verification**: Payment status is fetched directly from `https://api.paystack.co/transaction/verify/${ref}`. Client-supplied amounts and user IDs are ignored; plan and amount are matched against server table `plan_prices`.\n- **Idempotency**: `apply_verified_subscription_payment` uses `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(p_provider||':'||p_payment_reference,0))` and checks `billing_payments` to ensure duplicate webhook deliveries return `idempotent_replay: true` without granting credits twice.\n\n---\n\n### 14. Security Findings\n- Users cannot manipulate deductions: credit calculations and balance mutations take place exclusively inside PostgreSQL `SECURITY DEFINER` RPCs.\n- Negative balances are mathematically impossible: `v_user_balance >= v_effective_cost` is evaluated before any deduction, accompanied by row-level locks on `profiles`.\n- Plan gating enforces `billing_plan_rank(profile.plan) >= billing_plan_rank(rate.min_plan)` preventing unpaid users from executing Pro-only tools.\n\n---\n\n### 15.      1 // Aggressive Detector Service — isolated adapter for the SEO Assistant's
     2 // AI risk analysis engine (analyzeAIRisk from analysisEngine.ts).
     3 //
     4 // CRITICAL: This adapter calls analyzeAIRisk() without modification.
     5 // Do NOT alter, wrap, or override the underlying SEO Assistant logic.
     6 // The SEO Assistant page, its files, and its behavior are completely untouched.
     7 //
     8 // The SEO Assistant engine returns only two categories (AI + Human), plus a
     9 // risk level and recommendations. There is NO Mixed category — do not invent one.
    10 
    11 import { analyzeAIRisk } from '@/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';
    12 
    13 export interface AggressiveDetectorResult {
    14   // Scores (0–100) — exactly two categories, as returned by analyzeAIRisk.
    15   ai: number;
    16   human: number;
    17   // No 'mixed' field: the aggressive engine does not return a Mixed category.
    18   risk: 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High';
    19   // Recommendations returned as-is from the underlying engine.
    20   recommendations: string[];
    21 }
    22 
    23 export interface AggressiveDetectorError {
    24   message: string;
    25   canRetry: boolean;
    26 }
    27 
    28 // runAggressiveDetector calls the SEO Assistant's analyzeAIRisk function.
    29 // The text is passed unchanged — no preprocessing.
    30 export async function runAggressiveDetector(
    31   text: string
    32 ): Promise<AggressiveDetectorResult> {
    33   // analyzeAIRisk is synchronous but we wrap in a Promise to keep the
    34   // orchestration layer uniform (both engines return Promise).
    35   const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);
    36   return {
    37     ai: raw.aiScore,
    38     human: raw.humanScore,
    39     risk: raw.riskLevel,
    40     recommendations: raw.recommendations,
    41   };
    42 }
    43

   967 export function analyzeUniqueness(text: string): UniquenessResult {
   968   const words = getWords(text.toLowerCase());
   969   const totalWords = words.length || 1;
   970   const freq: Record<string, number> = {};
   971   for (const w of words) {
   972     if (w.length > 4) freq[w] = (freq[w] || 0) + 1;
   973   }
   974 
   975   // Requirement 1: Overused Words measured by 1% to 2% of entire content (max 10 to 20 times per 1000 words)
   976   const overusedThreshold = Math.max(4, Math.round(totalWords * 0.018));
   977   const overusedWords = Object.entries(freq)
   978     .filter(([, count]) => count > overusedThreshold)
   979     .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
   980     .slice(0, 15)
